- ID: `TYPE-REFERENCE-BOOK.1.2.23.3.62.6`
  Status: `in_progress` / all bounded assessment/backend outcomes recorded; canonical full23.4 closeout separate
  Goal: Repair independently confirmed receiver-only String-to-Array-to-incompatible-Hash immediate-with guards.

  Dependencies: 62.5; frozen62/String19/current immediate-with/actual-kind authority and independent62.2 receiver-context observation.
  Reproduce: `Value:: I {order="";out=" a,b ".trim().split(",").set_key({order=cat(order,"K");"k"},{order=cat(order,"V");1}).with(){order=cat(order,"W");return("ran")};return([out,order])}`. Rust native/restored CompiledSpec/plan returns `["ran","KVW"]` before/after62.2 rather than rejecting incompatible Hash arguments/later body. Impact: forbidden operand/body side effects and wrong result. Exact producer/original inputs/runtime observations retained in free-string-rust-intake.json.gz.
  Root cause: Rust eval_receiver_with_trailing_block_chain starts kind_guarded_value false for the literal String root; trim/split do not activate it. Array-to-Hash arguments run through dynamic dispatch, followed by with. Free-source62.2 admission is deliberately separate. Reverify current public AST/runtime before repair; no guessed peer defects.
  Acceptance: Fresh dedicated failing-to-passing normal; preserve source once/order/snapshots/valid String-Array-select methods/legitimate null-empty with, errors and existing malformed/deferred/context/domain/copy/function/parser/schema policy. Qualify existing Perl unready context independently. Commit bounded backend repairs with focused checks.
